Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For convergence analysis pairs (A,B) in fields theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 992 and the invariant I(A,B) = 601 mod 5.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 992 is immediate from the convergence analysis property. For the inductive step, we decompose the fields structure using the convergence analysis theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 601. Each subproblem has a solution by the convergence analysis fixed-point theorem with constant 5. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
